       id, title, description, assignee_json, labels_json\n321\t    )\n322\t}\n323\t\n324\t/// Assert that an issue is a P0 epic.\n325\t///\n326\t/// Verifies:\n327\t/// - issue_type is Epic\n328\t/// assert!(!issue.title.is_empty(), \"{}: title must not be empty\", ctx);\n368\t}\n369\t\n370\t/// Assert P0 epic display formatting.\n371\t///\n372\t/// Verifies that the epic's priority displays as Common test infrastructure for integration tests.\n//!\n//! Provides TempWorkspace harness for creating isolated test workspaces\n//! with automatic cleanup.\n\nuse std::fs;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse tempfile::TempDir;\n\n/// A temporary workspace for testing.\n///\n/// Creates a .beads directory with config and database, automatically\n/// cleaned up when dropped.\npub struct TempWorkspace {\n    /// Temp directory that holds the workspace (cleaned up on drop)\n    pub temp_dir: TempDir,\n    /// Path to the .beads directory\n    pub beads_dir: PathBuf,\n    /// Path to the database file\n    pub db_path: PathBuf,\n    /// Path to the JSONL file\n    pub jsonl_path: PathBuf,\n}\n\nimpl TempWorkspace {\n    /// Create a new temporary workspace with bf configuration.\n    pub fn new() -> anyhow::Result<Self> {\n        let temp_dir = TempDir::new()?;\n        let beads_dir = temp_dir.path().join(\".beads\");\n        fs::create_dir(&beads_dir)?;\n\n        // Initialize workspace with bf config\n        bead_forge::config::init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\")?;\n\n        let metadata = bead_forge::config::load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n        let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n        let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(\"issues.jsonl\");\n\n        // Open storage to create the database file and initialize schema\n        let _storage = bead_forge::Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            temp_dir,\n            beads_dir,\n            db_path,\n            jsonl_path,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Create a workspace with a pre-existing JSONL file.\n    pub fn with_jsonl(jsonl_content: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Self> {\n        let ws = Self::new()?;\n        fs::write(&ws.jsonl_path, jsonl_content)?;\n        Ok(ws)\n    }\n\n    /// Create a workspace from a fixture file.\n    ///\n    /// Loads a JSONL snapshot from tests/fixtures/ and copies it into\n    /// the workspace. Use this for JSONL round-trip and schema compatibility tests.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    ///\n    /// * `fixture_name` - Name of the fixture file (e.g., \"forge-snapshot.jsonl\")\n    ///\n    /// # Example\n    ///\n    /// ```rust\n    /// let ws = TempWorkspace::from_fixture(\"forge-snapshot.jsonl\").unwrap();\n    /// ws.import_jsonl().unwrap();\n    /// assert_eq!(ws.count_beads().unwrap(), 8);\n    /// ```\n    pub fn from_fixture(fixture_name: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Self> {\n        let ws = Self::new()?;\n        let fixture_path = PathBuf::from(\"tests/fixtures\").join(fixture_name);\n\n        let fixture_content = fs::read_to_string(&fixture_path).map_err(|e| {\n            anyhow::anyhow!(\"Failed to read fixture {}: {}\", fixture_path.display(), e)\n        })?;\n\n        fs::write(&ws.jsonl_path, fixture_content)?;\n        Ok(ws)\n    }\n\n    /// Open the storage backend for this workspace.\n    pub fn storage(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bead_forge::Storage> {\n        bead_forge::Storage::open(&self.db_path)\n    }\n\n    /// Get the workspace path (parent of .beads).\n    pub fn workspace_path(&self) -> &Path {\n        self.temp_dir.path()\n    }\n\n    /// Load the workspace config.\n    pub fn config(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bead_forge::Config> {\n        bead_forge::config::load_config(&self.beads_dir)\n    }\n\n    /// Load the workspace metadata.\n    pub fn metadata(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bead_forge::Metadata> {\n        bead_forge::config::load_metadata(&self.beads_dir)\n    }\n\n    /// Import issues from JSONL into the database.\n    pub fn import_jsonl(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bead_forge::jsonl::ImportResult> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.sync_from_jsonl(&self.jsonl_path)\n    }\n\n    /// Export issues from database to JSONL.\n    pub fn export_jsonl(&self, dirty_only: bool) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.sync_to_jsonl(&self.jsonl_path, dirty_only)\n    }\n\n    /// Create a test bead with the given ID and title.\n    pub fn create_bead(&self, id: &str, title: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        let bead = bead_forge::Issue::new(id.to_string(), title.to_string(), \".\".to_string());\n        storage.create_issue(&bead)\n    }\n\n    /// Create a test bead from a fully-specified Issue, preserving issue_type,\n    /// priority, description, labels, and all other fields.\n    ///\n    /// Use this instead of `create_bead` whenever the test asserts on any field\n    /// beyond id/title — `create_bead` persists a default (task-type) Issue.\n    pub fn create_issue(&self, issue: &bead_forge::Issue) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.create_issue(issue)\n    }\n\n    /// Get a bead by ID.\n    pub fn get_bead(&self, id: &str) -> anyhow::Result<Option<bead_forge::Issue>> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.get_issue(id)\n    }\n\n    /// List all beads in the workspace.\n    pub fn list_beads(&self) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<bead_forge::Issue>> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.list_all_issues()\n    }\n\n    /// Count beads in the workspace.\n    pub fn count_beads(&self) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n        let storage = self.storage()?;\n        storage.count_issues()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Create a sample JSONL line for a bead.\npub fn sample_bead_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":2,\"type\":\"task\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":[],\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a sample closed bead JSONL line.\npub fn sample_closed_bead_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str, close_reason: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"closed\",\"priority\":2,\"type\":\"task\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T12:00:00Z\",\"closed_at\":\"2024-01-01T12:00:00Z\",\"close_reason\":\"{}\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":[],\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title, close_reason\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a bead with dependencies JSONL line.\npub fn sample_bead_with_deps_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str, deps: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let deps_json = serde_json::to_string(deps).unwrap();\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":2,\"type\":\"task\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":[],\"dependencies\":[{}]}}\"#,\n        id, title, deps_json\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a bead with labels JSONL line.\npub fn sample_bead_with_labels_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str, labels: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let labels_json = serde_json::to_string(labels).unwrap();\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":2,\"type\":\"task\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":{},\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title, labels_json\n    )\n}\n\n/// Assert that two JSONL strings represent semantically equal issues.\n///\n/// This handles minor formatting differences like whitespace.\npub fn assert_jsonl_eq(jsonl1: &str, jsonl2: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n    let issue1: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(jsonl1)?;\n    let issue2: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(jsonl2)?;\n\n    if issue1 != issue2 {\n        anyhow::bail!(\n            \"JSONL mismatch:\\n  Expected: {}\\n  Got:      {}\",\n            jsonl1,\n            jsonl2\n        );\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// P0 Epic Test Infrastructure\n// ============================================================================\n\n/// Create a sample P0 epic JSONL line with minimal fields.\n///\n/// P0 (Priority::CRITICAL = 0) is the highest priority level.\n/// This is the minimal viable P0 epic for testing.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `id` - Epic ID (e.g., \"bf-epic-001\")\n/// * `title` - Epic title\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_jsonl(\"bf-epic-001\", \"Critical infrastructure migration\");\n/// let ws = TempWorkspace::with_jsonl(&jsonl).unwrap();\n/// ```\npub fn sample_p0_epic_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":0,\"issue_type\":\"epic\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":[],\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a P0 epic JSONL line with description.\n///\n/// Use this for testing P0 epics with descriptive text.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `id` - Epic ID\n/// * `title` - Epic title\n/// * `description` - Epic description text\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_with_description_jsonl(\n///     \"bf-epic-002\",\n///     \"Security overhaul\",\n///     \"Complete security audit and fixes\"\n/// );\n/// ```\npub fn sample_p0_epic_with_description_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str, description: &str) -> String {\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"{}\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":0,\"issue_type\":\"epic\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":[],\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title, description\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a P0 epic JSONL line with labels.\n///\n/// Use this for testing P0 epics with categorization labels.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `id` - Epic ID\n/// * `title` - Epic title\n/// * `labels` - Array of label strings\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_with_labels_jsonl(\n///     \"bf-epic-003\",\n///     \"Database migration\",\n///     &[\"database\", \"migration\", \"critical\"]\n/// );\n/// ```\npub fn sample_p0_epic_with_labels_jsonl(id: &str, title: &str, labels: &[&str]) -> String {\n    let labels_json = serde_json::to_string(labels).unwrap();\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":0,\"issue_type\":\"epic\",\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":{},\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title, labels_json\n    )\n}\n\n/// Create a comprehensive P0 epic JSONL line with all metadata.\n///\n/// Use this for full round-trip testing of P0 epic serialization.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `id` - Epic ID\n/// * `title` - Epic title\n/// * `description` - Epic description\n/// * `assignee` - Optional assignee\n/// * `labels` - Array of label strings\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_full_jsonl(\n///     \"bf-epic-004\",\n///     \"API redesign\",\n///     \"Complete REST API overhaul\",\n///     Some(\"architect-team\"),\n///     &[\"api\", \"backend\", \"p0\"]\n/// );\n/// ```\npub fn sample_p0_epic_full_jsonl(\n    id: &str,\n    title: &str,\n    description: &str,\n    assignee: Option<&str>,\n    labels: &[&str],\n) -> String {\n    let assignee_json = assignee\n        .map(|a| serde_json::to_string(a).unwrap())\n        .unwrap_or(\"null\".to_string());\n    let labels_json = serde_json::to_string(labels).unwrap();\n    format!(\n        r#\"{{\"id\":\"{}\",\"title\":\"{}\",\"description\":\"{}\",\"design\":\"\",\"acceptance_criteria\":\"\",\"notes\":\"\",\"status\":\"open\",\"priority\":0,\"issue_type\":\"epic\",\"assignee\":{},\"created_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"updated_at\":\"2024-01-01T00:00:00Z\",\"source_repo\":\".\",\"labels\":{},\"dependencies\":[],\"comments\":[]}}\"#,\n        id, title, description, assignee_json, labels_json\n    )\n}\n\n/// Assert that an issue is a P0 epic.\n///\n/// Verifies:\n/// - issue_type is Epic\n/// - priority is CRITICAL (0)\n/// - title is non-empty\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `issue` - The issue to verify\n/// * `context` - Optional context string for error messages\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let epic = storage.get_issue(\"bf-epic-001\").unwrap().unwrap();\n/// assert_p0_epic(&epic, Some(\"Retrieved epic\"));\n/// ```\npub fn assert_p0_epic(issue: &bead_forge::Issue, context: Option<&str>) {\n    let ctx = context.unwrap_or(\"Issue\");\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        issue.issue_type,\n        bead_forge::IssueType::Epic,\n        \"{}: must be epic type, got {:?}\",\n        ctx,\n        issue.issue_type\n    );\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        issue.priority,\n        bead_forge::Priority::CRITICAL,\n        \"{}: must be P0 (CRITICAL), got P{}\",\n        ctx,\n        issue.priority.0\n    );\n\n    assert_eq!(\n        issue.priority.0, 0,\n        \"{}: priority value must be 0, got {}\",\n        ctx, issue.priority.0\n    );\n\n    assert!(!issue.title.is_empty(), \"{}: title must not be empty\", ctx);\n}\n\n/// Assert P0 epic display formatting.\n///\n/// Verifies that the epic's priority displays as \"P0\".\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `issue` - The issue to verify\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let epic = storage.get_issue(\"bf-epic-001\").unwrap().unwrap();\n/// assert_p0_epic_display(&epic);\n/// ```\npub fn assert_p0_epic_display(issue: &bead_forge::Issue) {\n    let display = format!(\"{}\", issue.priority);\n    assert_eq!(\n        display, \"P0\",\n        \"Priority must display as 'P0', got '{}'\",\n        display\n    );\n}\n\n/// Assert P0 epic JSON serialization.\n///\n/// Verifies that a P0 epic serializes to JSON with correct field values.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `issue` - The issue to serialize and verify\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let epic = create_test_p0_epic();\n/// assert_p0_epic_json_serialization(&epic);\n/// ```\npub fn assert_p0_epic_json_serialization(issue: &bead_forge::Issue) {\n    let json = serde_json::to_string(issue).unwrap();\n\n    // Verify epic type\n    assert!(\n        json.contains(r#\"\"issue_type\":\"epic\"\"#),\n        \"JSON must contain epic type, got: {}\",\n        json\n    );\n\n    // Verify P0 priority\n    assert!(\n        json.contains(r#\"\"priority\":0\"#),\n        \"JSON must contain priority 0, got: {}\",\n        json\n    );\n\n    // Verify no \"P0\" string (priority serializes as integer)\n    assert!(\n        !json.contains(r#\"\"priority\":\"P0\"\"#),\n        \"JSON must not contain string 'P0' for priority, got: {}\",\n        json\n    );\n}\n\n/// Seed a workspace with P0 epic test data.\n///\n/// Creates multiple P0 epics for testing list operations and filtering.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `workspace` - The TempWorkspace to seed\n/// * `count` - Number of P0 epics to create\n///\n/// # Returns\n///\n/// Vector of created epic IDs\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let ws = TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n/// let epic_ids = seed_p0_epics(&ws, 5);\n/// assert_eq!(epic_ids.len(), 5);\n/// ```\npub fn seed_p0_epics(workspace: &TempWorkspace, count: usize) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<String>> {\n    let mut epic_ids = Vec::new();\n\n    for i in 0..count {\n        let id = format!(\"bf-p0-epic-{:03}\", i);\n        let title = format!(\"P0 Test Epic #{}\", i);\n\n        let epic = bead_forge::Issue {\n            id: id.clone(),\n            title,\n            issue_type: bead_forge::IssueType::Epic,\n            priority: bead_forge::Priority::CRITICAL,\n            description: Some(format!(\"Test epic number {} with P0 priority\", i)),\n            ..Default::default()\n        };\n\n        workspace.create_issue(&epic)?;\n        epic_ids.push(id);\n    }\n\n    Ok(epic_ids)\n}\n\n/// Count P0 epics in a workspace.\n///\n/// Queries the workspace and returns the count of issues that are:\n/// - issue_type: Epic\n/// - priority: CRITICAL (0)\n///\n/// # Arguments\n///\n/// * `workspace` - The TempWorkspace to query\n///\n/// # Returns\n///\n/// Number of P0 epics found\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let ws = TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n/// seed_p0_epics(&ws, 3).unwrap();\n/// assert_eq!(count_p0_epics(&ws).unwrap(), 3);\n/// ```\npub fn count_p0_epics(workspace: &TempWorkspace) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n    let all_issues = workspace.list_beads()?;\n    let p0_epics = all_issues\n        .iter()\n        .filter(|i| {\n            i.issue_type == bead_forge::IssueType::Epic\n                && i.priority == bead_forge::Priority::CRITICAL\n        })\n        .count();\n\n    Ok(p0_epics)\n}\n\n/// P0 Epic fixture builder.\n///\n/// Builder pattern for creating P0 epic test fixtures with varying configurations.\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// let epic = P0EpicBuilder::new(\"bf-epic-001\", \"Test epic\")\n///     .with_description(\"Critical infrastructure update\")\n///     .with_labels(&[\"backend\", \"database\"])\n///     .with_assignee(\"team-lead\")\n///     .build();\n/// ```\npub struct P0EpicBuilder {\n    id: String,\n    title: String,\n    description: Option<String>,\n    assignee: Option<String>,\n    labels: Vec<String>,\n    status: bead_forge::Status,\n}\n\nimpl P0EpicBuilder {\n    /// Create a new P0 epic builder.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    ///\n    /// * `id` - Epic ID\n    /// * `title` - Epic title\n    pub fn new(id: impl Into<String>, title: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: id.into(),\n            title: title.into(),\n            description: None,\n            assignee: None,\n            labels: Vec::new(),\n            status: bead_forge::Status::Open,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Add a description to the epic.\n    pub fn with_description(mut self, description: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.description = Some(description.into());\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Add an assignee to the epic.\n    pub fn with_assignee(mut self, assignee: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        self.assignee = Some(assignee.into());\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Add labels to the epic.\n    pub fn with_labels(mut self, labels: &[&str]) -> Self {\n        self.labels = labels.iter().map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Set the epic status.\n    pub fn with_status(mut self, status: bead_forge::Status) -> Self {\n        self.status = status;\n        self\n    }\n\n    /// Build the P0 epic Issue.\n    pub fn build(self) -> bead_forge::Issue {\n        bead_forge::Issue {\n            id: self.id,\n            title: self.title,\n            description: self.description,\n            assignee: self.assignee,\n            labels: self.labels,\n            issue_type: bead_forge::IssueType::Epic,\n            priority: bead_forge::Priority::CRITICAL,\n            status: self.status,\n            ..Default::default()\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use bead_forge::{IssueType, Priority, Status};\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_temp_workspace_creation() {\n        let ws = TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n        assert!(ws.beads_dir.exists());\n        assert!(ws.db_path.exists());\n        // JSONL file is created on export, not on initialization\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_temp_workspace_create_bead() {\n        let ws = TempWorkspace::new().unwrap();\n        ws.create_bead(\"bf-test\", \"Test bead\").unwrap();\n\n        let bead = ws.get_bead(\"bf-test\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(bead.id, \"bf-test\");\n        assert_eq!(bead.title, \"Test bead\");\n        assert_eq!(bead.status.to_string(), \"open\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_temp_workspace_with_jsonl() {\n        let jsonl = sample_bead_jsonl(\"bf-test\", \"Test bead\");\n        let ws = TempWorkspace::with_jsonl(&jsonl).unwrap();\n\n        let result = ws.import_jsonl().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(result.imported, 1);\n\n        let bead = ws.get_bead(\"bf-test\").unwrap().unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(bead.id, \"bf-test\");\n        assert_eq!(bead.title, \"Test bead\");\n    }\n\n    // P0 Epic Infrastructure Tests\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_sample_p0_epic_jsonl() {\n        let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_jsonl(\"bf-epic-001\", \"Test Epic\");\n\n        let issue: bead_forge::Issue = serde_json::from_str(&jsonl).unwrap();\n\n        assert_eq!(issue.id, \"bf-epic-001\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.title, \"Test Epic\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.issue_type, IssueType::Epic);\n        assert_eq!(issue.priority, Priority::CRITICAL);\n        assert_eq!(issue.priority.0, 0);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_sample_p0_epic_with_description_jsonl() {\n        let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_with_description_jsonl(\n            \"bf-epic-002\",\n            \"Security Epic\",\n            \"Complete security audit\",\n        );\n\n        let issue: bead_forge::Issue = serde_json::from_str(&jsonl).unwrap();\n\n        assert_eq!(issue.id, \"bf-epic-002\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.title, \"Security Epic\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            issue.description,\n            Some(\"Complete security audit\".to_string())\n        );\n        assert_eq!(issue.issue_type, IssueType::Epic);\n        assert_eq!(issue.priority, Priority::CRITICAL);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_sample_p0_epic_with_labels_jsonl() {\n        let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_with_labels_jsonl(\n            \"bf-epic-003\",\n            \"Database Epic\",\n            &[\"database\", \"migration\", \"critical\"],\n        );\n\n        let issue: bead_forge::Issue = serde_json::from_str(&jsonl).unwrap();\n\n        assert_eq!(issue.id, \"bf-epic-003\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.labels, vec![\"database\", \"migration\", \"critical\"]);\n        assert_eq!(issue.issue_type, IssueType::Epic);\n        assert_eq!(issue.priority, Priority::CRITICAL);\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_sample_p0_epic_full_jsonl() {\n        let jsonl = sample_p0_epic_full_jsonl(\n            \"bf-epic-004\",\n            \"API Redesign\",\n            \"Complete REST API overhaul\",\n            Some(\"architect-team\"),\n            &[\"api\", \"backend\", \"p0\"],\n        );\n\n        let issue: bead_forge::Issue = serde_json::from_str(&jsonl).unwrap();\n\n        assert_eq!(issue.id, \"bf-epic-004\");\n        assert_eq!(issue.title, \"API Redesign\");\n        assert_eq!(\n            issue.description,\n            Some(\"Complete REST API overhaul\".to_string())\n        );\n        assert_eq!(issue.assignee, Some(\"architect-team\".to_string()));\n        assert_eq!(issue.labels, vec![\"api\", \"backend\", \"p0\"]);\n Number of beads exported\n35\tpub fn flush(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n36\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n37\t        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n38\t    })?;\n39\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n40\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n41\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n42\t\n43\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n44\t\n45\t    // Get all issues for export\n46\t    let issues = storage.list_all_issues()?;\n47\t\n48\t    // Pre-export history backup: snapshot the outgoing issues.jsonl before it\n49\t    // is overwritten (best-effort — a failed backup must not abort the flush).\n50\t    let config = load_config(&beads_dir).unwrap_or_default();\n51\t    if let Err(e) = backup_before_export(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path, &config.history) {\n52\t        eprintln!(\"WARNING: pre-export history backup failed: {e}\");\n53\t    }\n54\t\n55\t    // Export to JSONL with atomic temp+rename\n56\t    let result = export_jsonl(&jsonl_path, || Ok(issues.clone()))?;\n57\t\n58\t    // Update export_hashes for all exported issues\n59\t    update_export_hashes_for_issues(&storage, &issues)?;\n60\t\n61\t    // Clear dirty marks - all beads have been flushed to JSONL\n62\t    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n63\t\n64\t    // Refresh the merge anchor: the freshly-exported JSONL is now the common\n65\t    // ancestor for the next three-way merge across checkouts.\n66\t    if let Err(e) = update_base_anchor(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path) {\n67\t        eprintln!(\"WARNING: could not update merge anchor: {e}\");\n68\t    }\n69\t\n70\t    Ok(result.count)\n71\t}\n72\t\n73\t/// Flush only dirty beads from SQLite to JSONL.\n74\t///\n75\t/// Incremental export for faster sync on large workspaces. Only exports\n76\t/// beads that have been modified since the last flush.\n77\t///\n78\t/// NOTE: This function exports ONLY dirty beads to the JSONL file, replacing\n79\t/// its contents. For a full export of all beads, use `flush()` instead.\n80\t///\n81\t/// # Arguments\n82\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n83\t///\n84\t/// # Returns\n85\t/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads exported\n86\tpub fn flush_dirty(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n87\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n88\t        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n89\t    })?;\n90\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n91\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n92\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n93\t\n94\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n95\t\n96\t    // Get dirty issues for export\n97\t    let dirty_issues = storage.list_dirty_issues()?;\n98\t    if dirty_issues.is_empty() {\n99\t        return Ok(0);\n100\t    }\n101\t\n102\t    // Export to JSONL with atomic temp+rename (only dirty issues)\n103\t    let result = export_jsonl_dirty(\n104\t        &jsonl_path,\n105\t        || Ok(dirty_issues.clone()),\n106\t        || storage.clear_dirty(),\n107\t    )?;\n108\t\n109\t    // Update export_hashes for dirty issues only\n110\t    update_export_hashes_for_issues(&storage, &dirty_issues)?;\n111\t\n112\t    Ok(result.count)\n113\t}\n114\t\n115\t/// Import beads from JSONL into SQLite.\n116\t///\n117\t/// Compares each bead in JSONL with SQLite state using content_hash.\n118\t/// INSERTs new beads, UPDATEs changed beads, SKIPs unchanged beads.\n119\t///\n120\t/// Collision resolution: when both JSONL and SQLite have changes for the\n121\t/// same bead, the one with the later `updated_at` timestamp wins.\n122\t///\n123\t/// NOTE: If there are unflushed beads (db-only or db-newer), this function\n124\t/// will emit a warning but proceed. The collision resolution logic preserves\n125\t/// SQLite versions when they are newer, so unflushed beads are protected.\n126\t/// Run `bf sync --flush-only` first to silence the warning.\n127\t///\n128\t/// # Arguments\n129\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n130\t///\n131\t/// # Returns\n132\t/// * `Ok(SyncResult)` - Import statistics\n133\tpub fn import(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<SyncResult> {\n134\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n135\t        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n136\t    })?;\n137\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n138\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n139\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n140\t\n141\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n142\t\n143\t    // Check for unflushed beads and warn\n144\t    let dirty_issues = storage.list_dirty_issues()?;\n145\t    if !dirty_issues.is_empty() {\n146\t        eprintln!(\n147\t            \"WARNING: {} unflushed bead(s) exist in SQLite (modified/created since last flush to JSONL).\",\n148\t            dirty_issues.len()\n149\t        );\n150\t        eprintln!(\"  Import will preserve SQLite versions when they are newer.\");\n151\t        eprintln!(\"  Run 'bf sync --flush-only' first to flush these beads to JSONL.\");\n152\t        let dirty_ids: Vec<String> = dirty_issues.iter().map(|i| i.id.clone()).collect();\n153\t        if dirty_ids.len() <= 5 {\n154\t            eprintln!(\"  Unflushed: {}\", dirty_ids.join(\", \"));\n155\t        } else {\n156\t            eprintln!(\n157\t                \"  Unflushed: {}, ... and {} more\",\n158\t                dirty_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n159\t                dirty_ids.len() - 5\n160\t            );\n161\t        }\n162\t    }\n163\t\n164\t    // Stream import with content_hash comparison\n165\t    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n166\t        import_jsonl(&jsonl_path, |issue| {\n167\t            let incoming_hash = issue.content_hash();\n168\t            let existing = Storage::get_issue_tx(tx, &issue.id)?;\n169\t\n170\t            match existing {\n171\t                None => {\n172\t                    // New bead - insert\n173\t                    Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n174\t                    Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n175\t                }\n176\t                Some(existing_issue) => {\n177\t                    let existing_hash = existing_issue.content_hash();\n178\t\n179\t                    if incoming_hash == existing_hash {\n180\t                        // Content unchanged - skip\n181\t                        Ok(UpsertResult::Unchanged)\n182\t                    } else {\n183\t                        // Content changed - use deterministic collision resolution\n184\t                        // The bead with the later updated_at wins\n185\t                        if issue.updated_at > existing_issue.updated_at {\n186\t                            Storage::update_issue_from_json_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n187\t                            Ok(UpsertResult::Updated)\n188\t                        } else {\n189\t                            // SQLite version is newer - skip JSONL version\n190\t                            Ok(UpsertResult::Unchanged)\n191\t                        }\n192\t                    }\n193\t                }\n194\t            }\n195\t        })\n196\t    })?;\n197\t\n198\t    // Rebuild blocked cache after import\n199\t    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n200\t\n201\t    // Clear dirty marks - after import from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n202\t    // Beads that were created/updated during import are now flushed to JSONL\n203\t    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n204\t\n205\t    // The JSONL we just imported is now the agreed-upon common state; record it\n206\t    // as the merge anchor for the next divergence.\n207\t    if let Err(e) = update_base_anchor(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path) {\n208\t        eprintln!(\"WARNING: could not update merge anchor: {e}\");\n209\t    }\n210\t\n211\t    Ok(SyncResult {\n212\t        imported: result.imported,\n213\t        exported: 0,\n214\t        updated: result.updated,\n215\t        skipped: result.skipped,\n216\t    })\n217\t}\n218\t\n219\t/// Full sync: import then flush.\n220\t///\n221\t/// Performs both import and flush operations. Use this when you want to\n222\t/// ensure bidirectional synchronization.\n223\t///\n224\t/// # Arguments\n225\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n226\t///\n227\t/// # Returns\n228\t/// * `Ok(SyncResult)` - Sync statistics\n229\tpub fn sync(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<SyncResult> {\n230\t    let import_result = import(workspace_dir)?;\n231\t    let exported = flush(workspace_dir)?;\n232\t\n233\t    Ok(SyncResult {\n234\t        imported: import_result.imported,\n235\t        exported,\n236\t        updated: import_result.updated,\n237\t        skipped: import_result.skipped,\n238\t    })\n239\t}\n240\t\n241\t/// Update export_hashes table for a set of issues.\n242\t///\n243\t/// This tracks which beads have been exported and their content hashes,\n244\t/// enabling incremental export operations.\n245\tfn update_export_hashes_for_issues(storage: &Storage, issues: &[Issue]) -> Result<()> {\n246\t    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n247\t        let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();\n248\t\n249\t        for issue in issues {\n250\t            let hash = issue.content_hash();\n251\t            tx.execute(\n252\t                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO export_hashes (issue_id, content_hash, exported_at)\n253\t                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)\",\n254\t                rusqlite::params![&issue.id, &hash, &now],\n255\t            )?;\n256\t        }\n257\t\n258\t        Ok(())\n259\t    })\n260\t}\n261\t\n262\t/// Find the .beads directory for a workspace.\n263\tpub fn find_workspace(start_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n264\t    find_beads_dir(start_dir)\n265\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", start_dir.display()))\n266\t}\n267\t\n268\t/// Get the JSONL path for a workspace.\n269\tpub fn get_jsonl_path(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n270\t    let beads_dir = find_workspace(workspace_dir)?;\n271\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n272\t    Ok(beads_dir.join(metadata.jsonl_export))\n273\t}\n274\t\n275\t/// Get the database path for a workspace.\n276\tpub fn get_db_path(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n277\t    let beads_dir = find_workspace(workspace_dir)?;\n278\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n279\t    Ok(beads_dir.join(metadata.database))\n280\t}\n281\t\n282\t#[cfg(test)]\n283\tmod tests {\n284\t    use super::*;\n285\t    use crate::config::init_workspace;\n286\t    use crate::model::{Issue, Sync protocol for bead-forge.\n//!\n//! Implements flush (SQLite → JSONL) and import (JSONL → SQLite) operations\n//! for git-backed bead synchronization.\n\nuse crate::config::{find_beads_dir, load_config, load_metadata};\nuse crate::history::backup_before_export;\nuse crate::jsonl::{export_jsonl, export_jsonl_dirty, import_jsonl, UpsertResult};\nuse crate::merge::update_base_anchor;\nuse crate::model::Issue;\nuse crate::storage::Storage;\nuse anyhow::Result;\nuse chrono::Utc;\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\n\n/// Sync operation results.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct SyncResult {\n    pub imported: usize,\n    pub exported: usize,\n    pub updated: usize,\n    pub skipped: usize,\n}\n\n/// Flush all beads from SQLite to JSONL.\n///\n/// This is the primary export operation for git commit. Exports all beads\n/// sorted by ID for stable diffs.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads exported\npub fn flush(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n    })?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Get all issues for export\n    let issues = storage.list_all_issues()?;\n\n    // Pre-export history backup: snapshot the outgoing issues.jsonl before it\n    // is overwritten (best-effort — a failed backup must not abort the flush).\n    let config = load_config(&beads_dir).unwrap_or_default();\n    if let Err(e) = backup_before_export(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path, &config.history) {\n        eprintln!(\"WARNING: pre-export history backup failed: {e}\");\n    }\n\n    // Export to JSONL with atomic temp+rename\n    let result = export_jsonl(&jsonl_path, || Ok(issues.clone()))?;\n\n    // Update export_hashes for all exported issues\n    update_export_hashes_for_issues(&storage, &issues)?;\n\n    // Clear dirty marks - all beads have been flushed to JSONL\n    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n\n    // Refresh the merge anchor: the freshly-exported JSONL is now the common\n    // ancestor for the next three-way merge across checkouts.\n    if let Err(e) = update_base_anchor(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path) {\n        eprintln!(\"WARNING: could not update merge anchor: {e}\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(result.count)\n}\n\n/// Flush only dirty beads from SQLite to JSONL.\n///\n/// Incremental export for faster sync on large workspaces. Only exports\n/// beads that have been modified since the last flush.\n///\n/// NOTE: This function exports ONLY dirty beads to the JSONL file, replacing\n/// its contents. For a full export of all beads, use `flush()` instead.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads exported\npub fn flush_dirty(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n    })?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Get dirty issues for export\n    let dirty_issues = storage.list_dirty_issues()?;\n    if dirty_issues.is_empty() {\n        return Ok(0);\n    }\n\n    // Export to JSONL with atomic temp+rename (only dirty issues)\n    let result = export_jsonl_dirty(\n        &jsonl_path,\n        || Ok(dirty_issues.clone()),\n        || storage.clear_dirty(),\n    )?;\n\n    // Update export_hashes for dirty issues only\n    update_export_hashes_for_issues(&storage, &dirty_issues)?;\n\n    Ok(result.count)\n}\n\n/// Import beads from JSONL into SQLite.\n///\n/// Compares each bead in JSONL with SQLite state using content_hash.\n/// INSERTs new beads, UPDATEs changed beads, SKIPs unchanged beads.\n///\n/// Collision resolution: when both JSONL and SQLite have changes for the\n/// same bead, the one with the later `updated_at` timestamp wins.\n///\n/// NOTE: If there are unflushed beads (db-only or db-newer), this function\n/// will emit a warning but proceed. The collision resolution logic preserves\n/// SQLite versions when they are newer, so unflushed beads are protected.\n/// Run `bf sync --flush-only` first to silence the warning.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(SyncResult)` - Import statistics\npub fn import(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<SyncResult> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir).ok_or_else(|| {\n        anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display())\n    })?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Check for unflushed beads and warn\n    let dirty_issues = storage.list_dirty_issues()?;\n    if !dirty_issues.is_empty() {\n        eprintln!(\n            \"WARNING: {} unflushed bead(s) exist in SQLite (modified/created since last flush to JSONL).\",\n            dirty_issues.len()\n        );\n        eprintln!(\"  Import will preserve SQLite versions when they are newer.\");\n        eprintln!(\"  Run 'bf sync --flush-only' first to flush these beads to JSONL.\");\n        let dirty_ids: Vec<String> = dirty_issues.iter().map(|i| i.id.clone()).collect();\n        if dirty_ids.len() <= 5 {\n            eprintln!(\"  Unflushed: {}\", dirty_ids.join(\", \"));\n        } else {\n            eprintln!(\n                \"  Unflushed: {}, ... and {} more\",\n                dirty_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n                dirty_ids.len() - 5\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Stream import with content_hash comparison\n    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        import_jsonl(&jsonl_path, |issue| {\n            let incoming_hash = issue.content_hash();\n            let existing = Storage::get_issue_tx(tx, &issue.id)?;\n\n            match existing {\n                None => {\n                    // New bead - insert\n                    Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n                    Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n                }\n                Some(existing_issue) => {\n                    let existing_hash = existing_issue.content_hash();\n\n                    if incoming_hash == existing_hash {\n                        // Content unchanged - skip\n                        Ok(UpsertResult::Unchanged)\n                    } else {\n                        // Content changed - use deterministic collision resolution\n                        // The bead with the later updated_at wins\n                        if issue.updated_at > existing_issue.updated_at {\n                            Storage::update_issue_from_json_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n                            Ok(UpsertResult::Updated)\n                        } else {\n                            // SQLite version is newer - skip JSONL version\n                            Ok(UpsertResult::Unchanged)\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        })\n    })?;\n\n    // Rebuild blocked cache after import\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    // Clear dirty marks - after import from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n    // Beads that were created/updated during import are now flushed to JSONL\n    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n\n    // The JSONL we just imported is now the agreed-upon common state; record it\n    // as the merge anchor for the next divergence.\n    if let Err(e) = update_base_anchor(&beads_dir, &jsonl_path) {\n        eprintln!(\"WARNING: could not update merge anchor: {e}\");\n    }\n\n    Ok(SyncResult {\n        imported: result.imported,\n        exported: 0,\n        updated: result.updated,\n        skipped: result.skipped,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Full sync: import then flush.\n///\n/// Performs both import and flush operations. Use this when you want to\n/// ensure bidirectional synchronization.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(SyncResult)` - Sync statistics\npub fn sync(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<SyncResult> {\n    let import_result = import(workspace_dir)?;\n    let exported = flush(workspace_dir)?;\n\n    Ok(SyncResult {\n        imported: import_result.imported,\n        exported,\n        updated: import_result.updated,\n        skipped: import_result.skipped,\n    })\n}\n\n/// Update export_hashes table for a set of issues.\n///\n/// This tracks which beads have been exported and their content hashes,\n/// enabling incremental export operations.\nfn update_export_hashes_for_issues(storage: &Storage, issues: &[Issue]) -> Result<()> {\n    storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        let now = Utc::now().to_rfc3339();\n\n        for issue in issues {\n            let hash = issue.content_hash();\n            tx.execute(\n                \"INSERT OR REPLACE INTO export_hashes (issue_id, content_hash, exported_at)\n                 VALUES (?1, ?2, ?3)\",\n                rusqlite::params![&issue.id, &hash, &now],\n            )?;\n        }\n\n        Ok(())\n    })\n}\n\n/// Find the .beads directory for a workspace.\npub fn find_workspace(start_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n    find_beads_dir(start_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", start_dir.display()))\n}\n\n/// Get the JSONL path for a workspace.\npub fn get_jsonl_path(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n    let beads_dir = find_workspace(workspace_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    Ok(beads_dir.join(metadata.jsonl_export))\n}\n\n/// Get the database path for a workspace.\npub fn get_db_path(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {\n    let beads_dir = find_workspace(workspace_dir)?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    Ok(beads_dir.join(metadata.database))\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use Auto-flush plumbing (Phase 7.1, child 1/5).\n//!\n//! This is the PLUMBING layer that later Phase 7.1 children consume. It does\n//! not change any command's behavior on its own — it only provides:\n//!\n//! * [`enabled`] — the effective-enablement resolution\n//!   (`config.sync.auto_flush && !--no-auto-flush`).\n//! * [`after_mutation`] / [`after_mutation_with_config`] — run the dirty flush\n//!   and report the outcome so callers can bridge a failure into a user-facing\n//!   warning (see [`crate::format::with_warning`] / [`crate::format::warn_stderr`]).\n//!\n//! The actual SQLite → JSONL export reuses [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`], so\n//! auto-flush and the explicit `bf sync --flush-only` path share one code path.\n\nuse crate::config::Config;\nuse crate::sync::flush_dirty;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Resolve whether auto-flush is effectively enabled for this invocation.\n///\n/// Two switches gate it: the persistent `sync.auto_flush` config master switch\n/// (default `true`) AND the absence of the per-invocation `--no-auto-flush`\n/// CLI override. Either one being off disables auto-flush.\npub fn enabled(config: &Config, no_auto_flush_flag: bool) -> bool {\n    config.sync.auto_flush && !no_auto_flush_flag\n}\n\n/// Outcome of an auto-flush attempt, ready to bridge into the warning channel.\n///\n/// Only [`FlushOutcome::Failed`] surfaces a warning — a disabled or successful\n/// flush is silent (`warning()` returns `None`).\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum FlushOutcome {\n    /// Auto-flush was disabled (config off or `--no-auto-flush`); nothing ran.\n    Disabled,\n    /// Flush ran successfully; carries the number of beads written to JSONL.\n    Flushed(usize),\n    /// Flush was attempted but failed; carries the human-readable warning.\n    Failed(String),\n}\n\nimpl FlushOutcome {\n    /// The warning string to surface (JSON `warning` key / stderr), if any.\n    ///\n    /// `Disabled` and `Flushed` are silent; only `Failed` yields `Some`.\n    pub fn warning(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Failed(msg) => Some(msg.as_str()),\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Flushed(_) => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Number of beads flushed, or `0` when disabled/failed.\n    pub fn flushed_count(&self) -> usize {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Flushed(n) => *n,\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Failed(_) => 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the flush was attempted and failed.\n    pub fn is_failure(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, FlushOutcome::Failed(_))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Perform the dirty-only flush of `workspace_dir`'s beads to JSONL.\n///\n/// Thin wrapper over [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`]; returns the number of beads\n/// written (`0` when nothing was dirty).\npub fn run(workspace_dir: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n    flush_dirty(workspace_dir)\n}\n\n/// Run auto-flush after a mutation, honoring a pre-resolved `enabled` decision,\n/// and return an outcome whose [`FlushOutcome::warning`] bridges into the\n/// JSON/stderr warning channel.\n///\n/// A failed flush is intentionally non-fatal: the mutation already succeeded,\n/// so the caller degrades to a warning rather than an error.\npub fn after_mutation(workspace_dir: &Path, enabled: bool) -> FlushOutcome {\n    if !enabled {\n        return FlushOutcome::Disabled;\n    }\n    match run(workspace_dir) {\n        Ok(count) => FlushOutcome::Flushed(count),\n        Err(e) => FlushOutcome::Failed(format!(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed: {e}\")),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience wrapper that resolves enablement from `config` + the CLI flag\n/// via [`enabled`] before delegating to [`after_mutation`].\npub fn after_mutation_with_config(\n    workspace_dir: &Path,\n    config: &Config,\n    no_auto_flush_flag: bool,\n) -> FlushOutcome {\n    after_mutation(workspace_dir, enabled(config, no_auto_flush_flag))\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::config::{init_workspace, Config};\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    fn config_with_auto_flush(value: bool) -> Config {\n        let mut cfg = Config::default();\n        cfg.sync.auto_flush = value;\n        cfg\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn enabled_true_only_when_config_on_and_flag_off() {\n        assert!(enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), false));\n        // CLI override wins over an enabled config.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), true));\n        // Config off disables regardless of the flag.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), false));\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), true));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_outcome_when_not_enabled() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), false);\n        assert!(matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Disabled));\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None, \"disabled flush must be silent\");\n        assert!(!outcome.is_failure());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn success_yields_no_warning() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(&beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\")).unwrap();\n\n        // A mutation marks the bead dirty, so the flush has something to write.\n        let issue = crate::model::Issue::new(\"bf-1\".into(), \"T\".into(), \".\".into());\n        storage.create_issue(&issue).unwrap();\n        storage.mark_dirty(\"bf-1\").unwrap();\n\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), true);\n        assert!(\n            matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Flushed(1)),\n            \"expected Flushed(1), got {outcome:?}\"\n        );\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None, \"successful flush must be silent\");\n        assert!(tmp.path().join(\".beads/issues.jsonl\").exists());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn forced_failure_yields_populated_warning() {\n        // No .beads directory anywhere → flush_dirty can't resolve a workspace,\n        // so the flush fails and the outcome carries a warning.\n        let tmp = TempDir::new_in(\"/tmp\").unwrap();\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), true);\n        assert!(outcome.is_failure(), \"expected failure, got {outcome:?}\");\n        let warning = outcome.warning().expect(\"failure must populate a warning\");\n        assert!(\n            warning.contains(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed\"),\n            \"warning must describe the auto-flush failure, got: {warning}\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn with_config_resolves_before_running() {\n        // Config disables → treated as Disabled even though the flag is off and\n        // a real workspace exists.\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n Auto-flush plumbing (Phase 7.1, child 1/5).\n//!\n//! This is the PLUMBING layer that later Phase 7.1 children consume. It does\n//! not change any command's behavior on its own — it only provides:\n//!\n//! * [`enabled`] — the effective-enablement resolution\n//!   (`config.sync.auto_flush && !--no-auto-flush`).\n//! * [`after_mutation`] / [`after_mutation_with_config`] — run the dirty flush\n//!   and report the outcome so callers can bridge a failure into a user-facing\n//!   warning (see [`crate::format::with_warning`] / [`crate::format::warn_stderr`]).\n//!\n//! The actual SQLite → JSONL export reuses [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`], so\n//! auto-flush and the explicit `bf sync --flush-only` path share one code path.\n\nuse crate::config::Config;\nuse crate::sync::flush_dirty;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Resolve whether auto-flush is effectively enabled for this invocation.\n///\n/// Two switches gate it: the persistent `sync.auto_flush` config master switch\n/// (default `true`) AND the absence of the per-invocation `--no-auto-flush`\n/// CLI override. Either one being off disables auto-flush.\npub fn enabled(config: &Config, no_auto_flush_flag: bool) -> bool {\n    config.sync.auto_flush && !no_auto_flush_flag\n}\n\n/// Outcome of an auto-flush attempt, ready to bridge into the warning channel.\n///\n/// Only [`FlushOutcome::Failed`] surfaces a warning — a disabled or successful\n/// flush is silent (`warning()` returns `None`).\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum FlushOutcome {\n    /// Auto-flush was disabled (config off or `--no-auto-flush`); nothing ran.\n    Disabled,\n    /// Flush ran successfully; carries the number of beads written to JSONL.\n    Flushed(usize),\n    /// Flush was attempted but failed; carries the human-readable warning.\n    Failed(String),\n}\n\nimpl FlushOutcome {\n    /// The warning string to surface (JSON `warning` key / stderr), if any.\n    ///\n    /// `Disabled` and `Flushed` are silent; only `Failed` yields `Some`.\n    pub fn warning(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Failed(msg) => Some(msg.as_str()),\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Flushed(_) => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Number of beads flushed, or `0` when disabled/failed.\n    pub fn flushed_count(&self) -> usize {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Flushed(n) => *n,\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Failed(_) => 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the flush was attempted and failed.\n    pub fn is_failure(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, FlushOutcome::Failed(_))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Perform the dirty-only flush of `workspace_dir`'s beads to JSONL.\n///\n/// Thin wrapper over [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`]; returns the number of beads\n/// written (`0` when nothing was dirty).\npub fn run(workspace_dir: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n    flush_dirty(workspace_dir)\n}\n\n/// Run auto-flush after a mutation, honoring a pre-resolved `enabled` decision,\n/// and return an outcome whose [`FlushOutcome::warning`] bridges into the\n/// JSON/stderr warning channel.\n///\n/// A failed flush is intentionally non-fatal: the mutation already succeeded,\n/// so the caller degrades to a warning rather than an error.\npub fn after_mutation(workspace_dir: &Path, enabled: bool) -> FlushOutcome {\n    if !enabled {\n        return FlushOutcome::Disabled;\n    }\n    match run(workspace_dir) {\n        Ok(count) => FlushOutcome::Flushed(count),\n        Err(e) => FlushOutcome::Failed(format!(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed: {e}\")),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience wrapper that resolves enablement from `config` + the CLI flag\n/// via [`enabled`] before delegating to [`after_mutation`].\npub fn after_mutation_with_config(\n    workspace_dir: &Path,\n    config: Auto-flush plumbing (Phase 7.1, child 1/5).\n//!\n//! This is the PLUMBING layer that later Phase 7.1 children consume. It does\n//! not change any command's behavior on its own — it only provides:\n//!\n//! * [`enabled`] — the effective-enablement resolution\n//!   (`config.sync.auto_flush && !--no-auto-flush`).\n//! * [`after_mutation`] / [`after_mutation_with_config`] — run the dirty flush\n//!   and report the outcome so callers can bridge a failure into a user-facing\n//!   warning (see [`crate::format::with_warning`] / [`crate::format::warn_stderr`]).\n//!\n//! The actual SQLite → JSONL export reuses [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`], so\n//! auto-flush and the explicit `bf sync --flush-only` path share one code path.\n\nuse crate::config::Config;\nuse crate::sync::flush_dirty;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Resolve whether auto-flush is effectively enabled for this invocation.\n///\n/// Two switches gate it: the persistent `sync.auto_flush` config master switch\n/// (default `true`) AND the absence of the per-invocation `--no-auto-flush`\n/// CLI override. Either one being off disables auto-flush.\npub fn enabled(config: &Config, no_auto_flush_flag: bool) -> bool {\n    config.sync.auto_flush && !no_auto_flush_flag\n}\n\n/// Outcome of an auto-flush attempt, ready to bridge into the warning channel.\n///\n/// Only [`FlushOutcome::Failed`] surfaces a warning — a disabled or successful\n/// flush is silent (`warning()` returns `None`).\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum FlushOutcome {\n    /// Auto-flush was disabled (config off or `--no-auto-flush`); nothing ran.\n    Disabled,\n    /// Flush ran successfully; carries the number of beads written to JSONL.\n    Flushed(usize),\n    /// Flush was attempted but failed; carries the human-readable warning.\n    Failed(String),\n}\n\nimpl FlushOutcome {\n    /// The warning string to surface (JSON `warning` key / stderr), if any.\n    ///\n    /// `Disabled` and `Flushed` are silent; only `Failed` yields `Some`.\n    pub fn warning(&self) -> Option<&str> {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Failed(msg) => Some(msg.as_str()),\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Flushed(_) => None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Number of beads flushed, or `0` when disabled/failed.\n    pub fn flushed_count(&self) -> usize {\n        match self {\n            FlushOutcome::Flushed(n) => *n,\n            FlushOutcome::Disabled | FlushOutcome::Failed(_) => 0,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Whether the flush was attempted and failed.\n    pub fn is_failure(&self) -> bool {\n        matches!(self, FlushOutcome::Failed(_))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Perform the dirty-only flush of `workspace_dir`'s beads to JSONL.\n///\n/// Thin wrapper over [`crate::sync::flush_dirty`]; returns the number of beads\n/// written (`0` when nothing was dirty).\npub fn run(workspace_dir: &Path) -> anyhow::Result<usize> {\n    flush_dirty(workspace_dir)\n}\n\n/// Run auto-flush after a mutation, honoring a pre-resolved `enabled` decision,\n/// and return an outcome whose [`FlushOutcome::warning`] bridges into the\n/// JSON/stderr warning channel.\n///\n/// A failed flush is intentionally non-fatal: the mutation already succeeded,\n/// so the caller degrades to a warning rather than an error.\npub fn after_mutation(workspace_dir: &Path, enabled: bool) -> FlushOutcome {\n    if !enabled {\n        return FlushOutcome::Disabled;\n    }\n    match run(workspace_dir) {\n        Ok(count) => FlushOutcome::Flushed(count),\n        Err(e) => FlushOutcome::Failed(format!(\"auto-flush to JSONL failed: {e}\")),\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience wrapper that resolves enablement from `config` + the CLI flag\n/// via [`enabled`] before delegating to [`after_mutation`].\npub fn after_mutation_with_config(\n    workspace_dir: &Path,\n    config: &Config,\n    no_auto_flush_flag: bool,\n) -> FlushOutcome {\n    after_mutation(workspace_dir, enabled(config, no_auto_flush_flag))\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::config::{init_workspace, Config};\n    use crate::storage::Storage;\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    fn config_with_auto_flush(value: bool) -> Config {\n        let mut cfg = Config::default();\n        cfg.sync.auto_flush = value;\n        cfg\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn enabled_true_only_when_config_on_and_flag_off() {\n        assert!(enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), false));\n        // CLI override wins over an enabled config.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(true), true));\n        // Config off disables regardless of the flag.\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), false));\n        assert!(!enabled(&config_with_auto_flush(false), true));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn disabled_outcome_when_not_enabled() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let outcome = after_mutation(tmp.path(), false);\n        assert!(matches!(outcome, FlushOutcome::Disabled));\n        assert_eq!(outcome.warning(), None, \"disabled flush must be silent\");\n        assert!(!outcome.is_failure());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn success_yields_no_warning() {\n        let tmp = TempDir::new().unwrap();\n        let beads_dir = tmp.path().join(\".beads\");\n        init_workspace(&beads_dir, \"bf\").unwrap();\n        let storage = Storage::open(&beads_dir.join(\"beads.db\")).unwrap();\n\n        // A mutation marks the bead dirty, so the flush has something to write.\n        let issue =
